# Crafting and recipes

Mechanics are identified from the 26.3 server source. Registry and resource entries are mechanically enumerated from the official reports, bootstrapped registries and built-in datapack.

| ID | Feature | Vanilla source | Test | Vanilla | Ferraloom |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| crafting-recipes.manager.reload | Decode recipe registries, reject duplicate or invalid entries and build recipe lookup indexes | `RecipeManager` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | partial |
| crafting-recipes.match.shaped-offset-mirror | Match shaped recipes at all valid offsets and mirrored orientations | `ShapedRecipe#matches; ShapedRecipePattern` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | pass |
| crafting-recipes.match.shapeless | Match shapeless ingredient multisets with exact occupied slot counts | `ShapelessRecipe#matches; StackedContents` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | pass |
| crafting-recipes.match.ingredients | Resolve item and tag ingredients against enabled features and current registries | `Ingredient` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | partial |
| crafting-recipes.result.components | Produce result components and preserve or copy only those required by the recipe | `Recipe#assemble; TransmuteRecipe` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | pass |
| crafting-recipes.remainders | Return crafting remainders into input slots, inventory or world drops | `CraftingRecipe#getRemainingItems; ResultSlot` | | - | todo |
| crafting-recipes.book.unlock | Unlock and remove recipes, track highlights and persist recipe-book state | `ServerRecipeBook` | `crafting::tests::book_add_remove_highlight_and_persistence_follow_displays` | pass | partial |
| crafting-recipes.book.placement | Place recipe ingredients into crafting grids with stacked ingredient accounting | `ServerPlaceRecipe` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_recipe_book_placement_inventory_and_grid` | pass | partial |
| crafting-recipes.book.limited-crafting | Restrict crafting to known recipes when limited crafting is enabled | `RecipeCraftingHolder` | | - | todo |
| crafting-recipes.furnace.types | Keep furnace, smoker, blast furnace and campfire recipe matching and timing separate | `AbstractCookingRecipe; AbstractFurnaceBlockEntity; CampfireBlockEntity` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | partial |
| crafting-recipes.special.transforms | Apply item-specific special recipe logic rather than generic shaped or shapeless matching | `RecipeSerializers` | `crafting::tests::vanilla_all_recipes_and_random_grids` | pass | pass |
